# Forms step 3c3, the questions: Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ask and store every answer Schedules D, E/F, G and H need from the filer (addresses, status, secured or not, the lien, who else is contacting them, which property secures a debt, lease kind and payment, "owe money now", codebtor links and H line 2), and refuse Complete and the forms calls while one is missing. No printed row changes yet.

**Architecture:**

- **Records.** New encrypted columns on `DebtInfo`, `ExecutoryContract` and `Codebtor`, a new one-to-one `ClaimAnswers`, and three plaintext status columns dropped, in one migration.
- **Saves.** The nested and list row serializers take every new answer, a half-filled row included. Links to other rows are checked against the filer's own session when saved, and a foreign or dead id saves as no link.
- **The wizard.** Amounts Owed, Contracts & Leases and Codebtors ask the questions and autosave half-filled rows. Each step disables Continue while its own copy of the server's gaps finds one.
- **Refusals.** `claim_step_gaps(session)` in `apps/intake/claims.py` lists the gaps in wizard order. Complete lists them, and `_prepare` raises the first one as a 422 for the forms calls.

**Tech Stack:** Django 5.0, DRF, `encrypted-model-fields`, pytest-django with sqlite (and Postgres for the lock test). React 19, TypeScript, vitest with MSW, Playwright for the journeys. No new dependency.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-06-forms-step3c-design.md` §7 (cited `§`), with §1, §2, §3.1 (S12, S15, S16, S20), §4, §11, §12 and §15. Where this plan and the spec disagree, the spec wins, except at "Rulings". `PS:<line>` cites the persona companion, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-forms-step3-personas.md`.

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## Global Constraints

- **What moves on a form.** No file under `data/forms/` changes, and `ASSERTED_CASES` and `known_defects.json` (`[]`) stay as they are.
  - What can change: G's and H's line 1 tick once a persona seeds a row (§4, B1); Priya's 106Sum line 2 (14,900, new) and 3c (71,900) once Kestrel seeds (S15); `has_*` ticks and totals as seeded rows change.
  - Nothing else on a printed page moves.
- **What is encrypted.** Every new column that holds a filer's answer:
  - The address columns, `outside_us`, the JSON columns, the yes/no and choice answers, and the contract's `monthly_payment`.
  - The `ClaimAnswers` row is encrypted too.
  - Not encrypted: the three foreign keys (`secured_by`, `vehicle_asset`, `owed_debt`) and `next_notice_party_id`. `contract_type` and `debt_type` stay plaintext, as they are today.
- **Logs and errors.** No log line, exception message or `__str__` carries a creditor's or party's name, an address or a last 4. A gap sentence names a creditor only in the reply body (§7.7).
- **Nothing pre-selected.** No radio, select or tick starts answered, with one exception: "Can the lender take back property…" starts from the debt type (U:77, B42, B43).
- **Copy.**
  - Information, never advice. Nothing tells the filer to estimate, and nothing suggests a property, a lien, a status or a figure (§1 anti-goals).
  - The form's own words go in `frontend/src/constants/formText.ts`, taken from the blank template's printed text (`form_b106d.pdf`, `form_b106ef.pdf`, `form_b106g.pdf`, `form_b106h.pdf`, `form_b106j.pdf` in `data/forms/pdfs/`), never from the field index's labels.
  - DigniFi's own sentences go in `frontend/src/constants/copy.ts`. A draft sentence carries `// DRAFT: counsel C-n` on the line above it (C-1, C-9, C-17, C-18 as §11 maps them).
  - Every gap sentence a backend test pins is one string literal, `export const NAME = '…';` or `"…"`. It holds no concatenation or template literal, and no quote of its own delimiter.
  - Every new `copy.ts` export is scored by `test/reading-level.test.ts`.
- **Tests.**
  - Named after outcomes, using synthetic data only: SSNs in the 900 range, phones 555-0100 to 555-0199, addresses on "Demo" streets or the persona companion's synthetic ones.
  - Security, encryption, UPL, IDOR and log-leak tests keep their assertions. A test that must change for a 3c3 field changes its setup, never what it asserts.
- **Backend tests.** Run each task's own files through `zsh /Users/controlroom/.claude/jobs/bb720722/tmp/sdd3b/py.sh -m pytest <path> --ds=config.settings.test -q -p no:cacheprovider`.
  - The helper does the `cd` and sources the environment. Never print env values.
  - Pass no `-c`, and give a run 600 s.
- **The lock test** needs Postgres.
  - Start the throwaway Postgres 14 with `zsh /Users/controlroom/.claude/jobs/bb720722/tmp/3c2-sdd/pgstart.sh`.
  - Run: `zsh /Users/controlroom/.claude/jobs/bb720722/tmp/3c2-sdd/pypg.sh -m pytest backend/apps/intake/tests/test_session_lock.py --ds=config.settings.test_postgres -m postgres -q -p no:cacheprovider`.
  - A skip under sqlite settings is not a pass.
- **Migration state:** `zsh …/sdd3b/py.sh backend/manage.py makemigrations --check --dry-run --settings=config.settings.test` must print `No changes detected`. CI has no such step, so the controller runs it after Task 1.
- **Frontend tests:**
  - Tests: `cd frontend && npx vitest run <files> --maxWorkers=2`.
  - Types: `cd frontend && npx tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit`, never `tsc -b`.
  - Style: `npx eslint <files>` and `npx prettier --check <files>`.
- **Whole suites and journeys.** Never run one from a task. The controller runs the full gates between tasks at load under 10, and one journey locally before the PR.
- **Git.**
  - Use `/usr/bin/git`, one plain command per call, on `feat/forms-step3c3`.
  - Stage by explicit path. Never stage `.claude/`, `.serena/`, `DESIGN.md`, `assets/`, `.impeccable` or `.superpowers/`.
  - Commit messages carry no `Co-Authored-By`, `Claude-Session` or other agent trailer.
  - No push, PR, merge or deploy.
- **Style.** Python lines run to 100 characters (`ruff==0.8.5`). Frontend files must pass prettier.

## Review Focus

These are five conditions the spec implies but §7.7 doesn't name, ordered by how likely they are to bite a filer. Each has its test in the owning task.

1. **The property a debt links to is removed on Assets.** The server's link goes null (`SET_NULL`). The wizard still holds the old id. The Amounts Owed step must show no property picked, and its gap must name the missing link, never keep showing a removed row as picked (Task 8, `shows no property picked once the linked row is removed on Assets`).
2. **A step 7 or 8 save where the list saves and the answer doesn't.** The step saves two requests. A failure of either must fail the save, keep the filer on the step and leave the next save to send both again (Task 7, `fails the contracts save when its answer does not save, and sends both again next time`).
3. **"Outside the US" ticked after a state and ZIP were typed.** The state and ZIP must clear and their boxes hide, so no forced letters reach a sworn form (Task 8, `clears the state and ZIP when the filer ticks outside the US`).
4. **A codebtor linked to a debt the filer then removes.** The server drops the dead link on read, but the wizard's held codebtor row still names it. The step must count it as no link, and the next save must not bring it back (Task 10, `counts a link to a removed amount owed as no link`).
5. **A state typed in lower case.** "il" saves as "IL" and passes Complete, as the debtor's own state does (Task 2, `test_a_state_typed_in_lower_case_saves_in_capitals`).

## Rulings

These settle what the spec leaves open, and every place this plan departs from it. Each names what it costs if it is wrong.

- **R1. One PR, fourteen tasks, refusals wired last.** The order is: records, saves, the gap function (Task 6, called by nothing yet), the wizard, the personas, then the wiring (Task 14).
  - So no task before Task 14 breaks a backend test that presses Complete or a forms call. When the wiring lands, the builders and the seed already pass `claim_step_gaps`.
  - The journeys break from Task 8, when Continue on Amounts Owed waits for an address, until Task 13 updates them. No journey runs before Task 13.
  - Cost if wrong: one large review. The final review is split by area (see "For the controller").
- **R2. One migration, `0040_claims_questions`.** It adds the new columns, creates `ClaimAnswers`, drops `is_contingent`, `is_unliquidated` and `is_disputed`, and drops the defaults on `contract_type` and `relationship` (B34).
  - The new columns hold no old data, so no copy step is needed.
  - Undone, the three dropped columns come back `False`, as their old default.
  - Cost if wrong: a rollback finds the three status columns `False`. No production code ever wrote them (§7.1).
- **R3. `outside_us` is `EncryptedBooleanField(default=False)` on the three tables**, a class the installed package already has (`encrypted_model_fields/fields.py:130`, in the venv, not `apps/intake/fields.py`). It is a boolean in the API and in the JSON of notice parties and `ClaimAnswers`.
  - Cost if wrong: one field class no other model uses yet.
- **R4. Yes/no and choice answers are `EncryptedCharField(null=True, blank=True)` with their choices**, as `Codebtor.relationship` already is. null is unanswered.
  - These are `home_payment_line`, `collateral_who`, `offset_answer`, `community_debt`, `lease_kind` and `owes_now`.
  - `community_debt` holds `"yes"` or `""` (B33). A tick has no unanswered state.
- **R5. The JSON shapes, stored as encrypted text and read by a `get_*` helper as `Household.members` is:**
  - `lien`: `{"kinds": [...], "other": "<text>"}`, kinds from `DebtInfo.LIEN_KINDS = ("agreement", "statutory", "judgment", "other")`, the form's four ticks in its order. null is unanswered.
  - `claim_status`: a list from `DebtInfo.CLAIM_STATUSES = ("contingent", "unliquidated", "disputed")`, kept in that order with duplicates dropped. `[]` is "None of these" and null is unanswered (B30).
  - `notice_parties`: a list of `{id, name, street_address, street_2, city, state, zip_code, outside_us, last_4}`. `[]` is No and null is unanswered.
    - Ids come from `next_notice_party_id` and are never reused, as `Household.store_members` does.
    - At most `DebtInfo.MAX_NOTICE_PARTIES = 10` per debt, a bound the spec doesn't give. Cost if wrong: an eleventh party is a 400.
  - `Codebtor.links`: a list of `{kind, id}`, kind from `("debt", "contract")`, duplicates dropped.
- **R6. Links are checked against the session when saved, and only on the session save and the list PUT.**
  - On the nested and list row serializers, `secured_by`, `vehicle_asset` and `owed_debt` are `IntegerField(source="<name>_id", required=False, allow_null=True)`.
  - `_save_rows_by_id(session, model, rows, scoped=None)` gains `scoped`, a map such as `{"secured_by_id": AssetInfo}`. An id that names no row of that model on this session saves as `None`.
  - A codebtor's `links` keep only entries that name one of the session's debts or contracts when saved, and drop dead ones on read (B9).
    - `scoped` covers FK columns only. So `CodebtorRowSerializer.validate` filters `links`, using the session `_save_list` passes in its context, and returns a list.
    - `_assign(row, data)` (Task 2) is the one place that writes JSON answers. It dumps `Model.JSON_ANSWERS`, stores `notice_parties` through `store_notice_parties`, and sets every other key.
  - The reply shows what was stored, so a foreign id is never echoed (B7).
  - **A dead link** is null, or a row now on another session, since `AssetViewSet` and `DebtViewSet` can move a row to another of the filer's sessions after the link was made.
    - On read, an FK whose row is on another session reads as null, and such a codebtor link is dropped.
    - Task 6's gaps treat it as no link, and so do the steps' copies: an id that isn't among the step's held rows.
  - On the base serializers (the per-item `POST`/`PATCH /debts/` and the single contract and codebtor `POST`) the link fields and `links` are read-only. No frontend code calls those paths.
  - The base `DebtInfoSerializer` also carries `lien`, `claim_status` and `notice_parties` read-only, since those paths never go through `_assign`. A dict saved there would be stored as its Python repr, and every later read of the session would fail. `NestedDebtInfoSerializer` is their only writer (Task 2).
  - Cost if wrong: a client of the per-item paths can't set a link or a JSON answer.
- **R7. A state saves as typed, upper-cased, up to two characters, or blank.** Complete counts it missing unless it is two letters by `DebtorInfoSerializer`'s own test (`len(value) == 2 and value.isalpha()`), and a ZIP missing when blank, except where `outside_us` is ticked (B38, B49).
  - The spec's "that check runs at Complete" is wrong: `validate_state` runs on a `debtor_info` save. That doesn't change this plan, which checks at Complete as B38 asks.
- **R8. Every gap is listed, row by row.** Complete lists every gap §7.5 names, in wizard order. A step's own copy shows only the first gap, live, in its `role="status"` region.
  - Within the Debts block: the step-level gap, then each row in `ordered_debts` order, with all of that row's gaps in §7.5's order (2 to 5).
  - Contracts and codebtors go in id order (B37).
  - A row is named by its name, else `one amount owed`, `one contract` or `one codebtor` (§7.5).
  - Cost if wrong: a long list on Review for a filer with many half-filled rows. The step shows one gap at a time.
- **R9. The draft sentence becomes the first Debts gap of `claim_step_gaps`.** `complete`'s inline literal goes, and the words stay as they are.
  - `_prepare` raises the first claim gap, so a forms call meets the draft sentence too. Unreachable in practice: an upload reopens a completed intake, and `_check_finished` refuses first.
- **R10. `ClaimStepGap(Exception)` carries its `step` per instance**, `ClaimStepGap(message, step)`, since claim gaps fix on four steps (`assets`, `debts`, `contracts`, `codebtors`). `_failure` already reads `getattr(exc, "step")`.
  - `claims.py` keeps "no model imports" (its docstring changes from "Pure: no models"). It reads the session's relations by attribute, as `asset_step_gaps` does.
- **R11. Drafts confirm on Continue.** `update_step` with `current_step == 7` clears every draft of the session, under its lock, right after `session.save()` and before any `data` the request carries is saved. So the rows that save loads, and the reply built from them, read confirmed. A refused `data` raises inside the atomic block and rolls the clearing back. `partial_update`'s clearing goes (§7.2).
  - Any client posting 7 confirms, as the spec accepts.
- **R12. The sentences are this plan's wording, except the three the spec gives** (the amount, the address and the same-vehicle overlap) and the existing draft sentence.
  - Task 6's gap table is the one source: each gap's order, condition, step and sentence.
  - Task 6 writes every sentence as a `copy.ts` constant and the server's `claim_step_gaps` from the same table, with `test_claim_gaps.py` pinning each pair.
  - Tasks 8–10 import the constants and copy the conditions from that table.
  - Two sentences were reworded to pass `reading-level.test.ts`'s grade 9 ceiling: `DEBT_GAP_STATUS` and `CODEBTOR_GAP_COMMUNITY`, both now questions or lists a filer can scan.
  - The Assets step's "Do you live here?" gap is the server's alone. It is on no step's copy (§7.5).
  - Because a debt's address may be ticked outside the US, the address gap gets a street-and-city form. Both forms get a nameless variant for debts, as §7.2 gives one.
  - Cost if wrong: copy the owner rewords.
- **R13. The UPL list for claims allows "$0" and nothing else with a dollar sign.**
  - The spec's amount sentence and the same-vehicle sentence say "$0", which the assets list's `/\$\s?\d/` would refuse.
  - `upl-claims-copy.test.ts` uses `/\$\s?(?!0\b)\d/` in its place, plus every other pattern of `upl-assets-copy.test.ts`.
  - "$0" names the form's floor, never a value to enter.
- **R14. The amount box keeps what the filer types, and shows a stored 0 as empty.**
  - The row holds the parsed figure, `undefined` while the box is blank. The body sends a blank amount as 0 (B29), so a loaded "0.00" and a blank box build the same body, and a visit alone sends nothing.
  - It listens with `onInput`, as `AssetRowFields` does, so a Backspace that empties it clears the figure.
  - Departs from §7.2's "loaded or typed": a typed 0 stays visible until the step is next shown, so the filer's keystroke never vanishes.
  - Cost if wrong: a typed "0" shows as "0" until the next visit.
- **R15. The "pressed" mark is `_securedPressed`, browser-only, like `_key`.**
  - A loaded row counts as pressed, unless it is a draft (B43). A new row counts as unpressed.
  - Every request strips the keys starting with `_` (`withoutKey` widens to all of them).
- **R16. Steps 7 and 8 save two requests.**
  - Step 7's body is `{contracts, claim_answers: {contracts_answer}}`. Step 8's is `{codebtors, claim_answers: {codebtors_answer, the H line 2 keys}}`.
  - `saveCurrentStepData` sends the list PUT, then a session PATCH carrying only `claim_answers`. Either failing fails the save.
  - Step 6's body adds `claim_answers: {no_debts}` to its one session PATCH.
  - Cost if wrong: one more request per save on steps 7 and 8.
- **R17. A list's body leaves out only an all-blank row with no id.** "All-blank" means every answer is empty: no text, no pick, no tick, no link.
  - So a stored row whose name is cleared is sent nameless and keeps its id, which closes 3c2's R17. The nested and list row serializers allow a blank name and type (§7.2).
- **R18. "I have no amounts owed" follows the household list's "No one else…".** It shows while no row is touched, and the body sends `no_debts: true` only while it is pressed and no row is touched. Otherwise the body sends null.
  - A filer can remove every row (B17): Remove shows on every row, and the step can hold none.
- **R19. Review keeps the order the wizard holds**, which is `ordered_debts`' order as the session read gave it, with rows added since then after them.
  - Departs from §7.6, which says Review uses `ordered_debts`' order. A browser copy of that sort would need its accent folding, and Review shows no line numbers that order could contradict.
  - Cost if wrong: a debt added this visit is listed last on Review instead of in its letter.
- **R20. `account_last_4` is `account_last_4(number)` in `claims.py`: the last four digits of the number's digits (fewer, if it has fewer), and "" for none.** 3c5 prints the same function's answer. It is a read-only `SerializerMethodField`.
- **R21. The date is bounded at `DebtInfo.DATE_INCURRED_MAX_LENGTH = 15` characters, measured (B11).**
  - The narrowest date box is D's "Date debt was incurred", 50.04 pt wide. E/F's are 62.8–67.9 pt.
  - At 6 pt, by `text_fit.fit`, it prints `00/0000-00/0000` whole (15 characters, hyphen). With an en dash, it prints 14 characters, `00/0000–00/000`.
  - So 15 takes a month-and-year range typed with a hyphen.
  - Cost if wrong: a 15-character range typed with an en dash saves, then is refused at Generate as `ValueTooLong`, with `step: "debts"`. The bound 14 would refuse `01/2020-06/2021` at save, though it prints.
- **R22. Lock order.**
  - The single contract and codebtor `POST` take the session lock before they validate, as the list PUT does since 3c2.
  - The order and lock tests' spies on `validate` wrap the real method, `wraps=` the original, since 3c3's row checks now read stored rows (handed over by 3c2).
- **R23. The choices stay class attributes**, and the frontend copies move to `frontend/src/constants/claims.ts`:
  - `DEBT_TYPE_WORDS`, `CONTRACT_TYPE_WORDS`, `CODEBTOR_RELATIONSHIP_WORDS` and `LEASE_KIND_WORDS`.
  - Pinned by `test_claims_frontend_copy.py` on their keys, in order.
  - `CODEBTOR_RELATIONSHIP_WORDS` is not the household's `RELATIONSHIP_WORDS`, a different set with other keys.
- **R24. The "Do you live here?" answer is `lives_here` in an A/B row's `details`, `"yes"`, `"no"` or null, asked on every line 1 row (shape `place`) and line 4 row (shape `vehicle_no_mileage`)** (B8). It is no Assets gap: it is a gap only once a debt links to the row (Task 13). Its words are DigniFi's, in `copy.ts`.
- **R25. Which property a link may name.**
  - A debt's picker lists every A/B row with an id.
  - A vehicle lease's picker lists the rows on lines 3 and 4 with an id, leaving out a row answered "I live here" (B16).
  - `collateral_who` is asked when the linked row's shape is none of `place`, `vehicle` and `vehicle_no_mileage`, the three shapes that ask `who` (`asset_lines.py:236-240`).
- **R26. `YesNoQuestion` moves out of `HouseholdList.tsx`, unchanged, to `components/wizard/steps/YesNoQuestion.tsx`, in Task 5.** Its answers are booleans, and `lives_here` and the claim answers are `"yes"`/`"no"` strings. So Task 5 adds a sibling, `YesNoField`, in the same file, with the same markup and string values, and Tasks 8–10 use it.

- **R27. Notice-party ids are not paired from the reply.**
  - A party typed in the browser has no id until the debt is read again. Each autosave sends it without one, the server gives it a fresh id, and the one it gave last time is dropped as missing.
  - No id is reused and no party is lost. The ids only climb.
  - Cost if wrong: `next_notice_party_id` grows by one per save of a new party. 3c5 and 3c6 never print it.
- **R28. Corrections to §7.7's pin list.**
  - `test_session_nested_save.py:304` stays. It asserts `is_draft` false on new rows, which still holds. Task 4 adds a sibling test that a scanned draft stays a draft in a PATCH reply.
  - `IntakeWizard.test.tsx:3294` ("sends nothing while a stored row's name is cleared") moves in Task 7, since autosave now runs while a step has a gap.
  - `:3604` and `:3619` move in Tasks 9 and 10, when the steps' own name rules go.
  - The persona dates (S16) become plain dates or ranges, each at most 15 characters: PS:183 `11/2025`, PS:422 `06/2015–2026`, PS:649 `2024–2025`. The longer wording stays in the companion as a note.
  - Tier 3 moves for Priya only: 106Sum line 2 (`14900.00`, a new key) and 3c (`71900.00`). Tier 3 covers no G or H box.

---

## For the controller

- **Dispatch extras.** A task's brief holds its own text only. Hand each implementer the Interfaces of the tasks it consumes, and the rulings it names.
- **Gates.** Run the full backend, Postgres lock, frontend and type gates after Tasks 4, 7, 11 and 14. Run `makemigrations --check` after Task 1, and one journey locally (`maria-happy-path`) after Task 13 and again after Task 14.
  - `test_data_migrations.py` takes about 600 s alone. Run the backend gate in the background, never as one foreground call.
  - Two Postgres lock runs on one server must name different databases in `DATABASE_URL`. They share the test database name otherwise, and both fail at setup ("database is being accessed by other users").
- **Final review.** Run it per area: records and saves (Tasks 1–5), the gaps (6), the wizard (7–11), personas and journeys (12–13), and the wiring (14). Then run one cross-area pass on the gap conditions (Task 6's table against Tasks 8–10's `*Gaps.ts`).

---

### Task 1: Records: the new columns, `ClaimAnswers`, and one migration

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/models.py` (`DebtInfo` at :762, `ExecutoryContract` at :1012, `Codebtor` at :1038; new `ClaimAnswers` after `AssetLineAnswers`, which ends at :750)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/migrations/0040_claims_questions.py` (latest is `0039_debtinfo_no_ordering`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_models.py:71-86` (`test_debt_status_flags` goes with the columns, §7.7)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_claims_records.py`
- Modify: `docs/security/data-protection.md:44-46` (the new encrypted columns)

**Interfaces:**

- Produces (every encrypted column below is a class from `encrypted_model_fields.fields`, or `apps.intake.fields.EncryptedDecimalField`):
  - `DebtInfo`
    - Address: `street_address` (`EncryptedCharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")`), `street_2` (255, same), `city` (100), `state` (2), `zip_code` (10), and `outside_us` (`EncryptedBooleanField(default=False)`, R3).
    - `secured_by`: `ForeignKey(AssetInfo, null=True, blank=True, on_delete=SET_NULL, related_name="secured_debts")`. `AssetInfo` is defined above `DebtInfo` (:634), so no string reference.
    - Answers (R4), each `EncryptedCharField(max_length=<n>, choices=…, null=True, blank=True)`:
      - `home_payment_line` (max 10, `HOME_PAYMENT_LINES = ("first", "additional")`)
      - `collateral_who` (max 20, `choices` from `asset_lines.WHO`)
      - `offset_answer` (max 3, `YES_NO`, the module constant at `models.py:15`; it is a flat tuple, `("yes", "no")`, so the model field takes `choices=[(v, v) for v in YES_NO]`, or `makemigrations` stops on fields.E005)
    - `community_debt`: `EncryptedCharField(max_length=3, choices=[("yes", "yes")], blank=True, default="")` (B33: `""` or `"yes"`, no unanswered state).
    - JSON (R5), each `EncryptedTextField(null=True, blank=True)`: `lien`, `claim_status`, `notice_parties`. Plus `next_notice_party_id = PositiveIntegerField(default=1)`.
    - Class attributes: `LIEN_KINDS = ("agreement", "statutory", "judgment", "other")`, `CLAIM_STATUSES = ("contingent", "unliquidated", "disputed")`, `MAX_NOTICE_PARTIES = 10`, `NOTICE_PARTY_KEYS = ("name", "street_address", "street_2", "city", "state", "zip_code", "outside_us", "last_4")`, and `JSON_ANSWERS = ("lien", "claim_status")` (the columns Task 2's `_save_rows_by_id` stores with `json.dumps`).
    - `DATE_INCURRED_MAX_LENGTH = 15` (R21, measured below). It lives on `DebtInfo`, since `serializers.py` already imports the model and `claims.py` is meant to stay model-free. The column stays `EncryptedTextField`.

Doesn't save.

Its keys are the seven text keys of `KEYS`.
- **R21, measured (this section's check phase).**
  - The narrowest date box on D or E/F is Schedule D's `Date debt was incurred` (and its four siblings `_2` to `_5`): 50.04 pt wide (page 1 `T31`, `T56`; page 2 `T35`, `T61`, `T87`), size 10, margin 1, no `/MaxLen`, not a comb. E/F's boxes are 62.8 to 67.9 pt, so they hold anything D's holds.
  - With `text_fit.fit(widget, "form_b106d.pdf", text)` (the call `PDFFormFiller._plan` makes, `pdf_filler.py:465`), at 6 pt:

    | string | characters | prints whole |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | `00/0000–00/0000` (en dash) | 15 | **no** (`None`) |
    | `00/0000–00/000` (en dash, one digit short) | 14 | yes, 6.0 pt |
    | `00/0000-00/0000` (hyphen) | 15 | yes, 6.0 pt |
    | `0000–0000` | 9 | yes, 9.0 pt |
    | `00/00/0000` | 10 | yes, 9.0 pt |

  - The en dash is wider than the hyphen (Helvetica 0.556 em against 0.333), which is why the hyphen form fits at 15 and the en dash form at 14. Fourteen zeros do not fit (thirteen do) and nine `W`s do not (eight do), so no character count is exact for free text.
  - **Ruling for the drafter's constant: 15.** It is the longest range shape that prints whole in the characters a filer is likely to type (`00/0000-00/0000`). A 15-character en dash range saves and then fails at Generate with `ValueTooLong`, which names the box (and links to the Amounts Owed step once 3c5 gives the binding its fix). Setting 14 instead would refuse the natural 15-character hyphen range at save. The cost of 15 is a late refusal for a filer who types an en dash and fills all 15 characters. Say so in the review if the owner prefers 14.
  - The record of the measurement is `test_the_date_bound_is_the_longest_range_the_narrowest_box_prints`, below, which re-measures from the template so a template change moves the test.

**Files:**

  - `_save_rows_by_id` (:617), which gains `scoped` (R6).
  - `IntakeSessionSerializer.update` (:957-958) passes `scoped` for debts, and `to_representation` (:967) selects `secured_by` with the debts.
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/claims.py` (add `account_last_4`, R20; the file is 17 lines today and holds `ordered_debts`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/documents/views.py:233-235` (`validate`: the `amount_owed` `DecimalField` gains `min_value=Decimal("0")`, B28; put `from decimal import Decimal` after `from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor` if it is absent, since ruff's I001 orders it there)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_debt_answers.py`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's fields, `DebtInfo.JSON_ANSWERS`, `store_notice_parties`, `DATE_INCURRED_MAX_LENGTH`.
- Produces:
  - **The base `DebtInfoSerializer`** (per-item `POST`/`PATCH /debts/`) lists every new column in `Meta.fields`, with:
    - `amount_owed` `DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, min_value=Decimal("0"))`, so a negative is a 400 on every path.
    - `secured_by`: `IntegerField(source="secured_by_id", read_only=True)` (R6: the per-item path can't set a link). `creditor_name` and `debt_type` stay required.
    - `account_last_4`: `SerializerMethodField`, `account_last_4(obj.account_number)`. `account_number` stays write-only.
    - The address, from `AddressAnswersMixin` (below): the base serializer is `class DebtInfoSerializer(AddressAnswersMixin, serializers.ModelSerializer)`, and the six names are in `Meta.fields`.
    - `home_payment_line`, `collateral_who`, `offset_answer` each `ChoiceField(choices=…, allow_null=True, required=False)`; `community_debt` `ChoiceField(choices=["yes"], allow_blank=True, required=False)`.
    - `date_incurred` `CharField(max_length=DebtInfo.DATE_INCURRED_MAX_LENGTH, allow_blank=True, allow_null=True, required=False)`.
    - `lien`, `claim_status` and `notice_parties`: the three field classes below, each declared `read_only=True` on the base (R6: the per-item path can't set them, see the R6 bullet below).
  - **`NestedDebtInfoSerializer`** (a session save) adds, over the base:
    - `id` writable (`IntegerField(required=False)`), as now.
    - `secured_by`: `IntegerField(source="secured_by_id", required=False, allow_null=True)`.
    - `lien`, `claim_status` and `notice_parties`: redeclared writable (`LienField(required=False, allow_null=True)` and the same for the other two). The nested row is the only path that writes them.
    - `creditor_name`: `CharField(max_length=255, required=False, allow_blank=True)`; `debt_type`: `ChoiceField(choices=DebtInfo.DEBT_TYPE_CHOICES, required=False, allow_blank=True)`. `amount_owed` stays required (the wizard sends a blank as 0, B29).
  - **Three field classes** in `serializers.py` (subclasses of `serializers.Field`; `get_attribute` calls the model's `get_<name>()` so the reply shows the parsed value, and `to_representation` returns it as it is):
    - `LienField`: null, or `{"kinds": [...], "other": str}`. `kinds` must each be in `DebtInfo.LIEN_KINDS`; stored in that order, duplicates dropped; `other` is `CharField(max_length=255, allow_blank=True)`, `""` when absent. An unknown kind is a 400.
    - `ClaimStatusField`: null, or a list of `DebtInfo.CLAIM_STATUSES` kept in that order, duplicates dropped; `[]` is "None of these". An unknown status is a 400.
    - `NoticePartiesField`: null, or a list of at most `DebtInfo.MAX_NOTICE_PARTIES` rows of a `NoticePartySerializer` (`id` optional int or null; `name` 255, `street_address` 255, `street_2` 255, `city` 100, `zip_code` 10, `last_4` 4, each `allow_blank`, `required=False`; `state` up to 2, upper-cased; `outside_us` bool). An eleventh row is a 400.
  - **The JSON answers are R6 too.** `DebtViewSet`'s per-item `POST` and `PATCH` save through `ModelSerializer.create`/`update`, never through `_save_rows_by_id`'s `_assign`. A `lien` sent there would reach the `EncryptedTextField` as a Python dict, which stores `str(value)` (single quotes), so `get_lien()`'s `json.loads` would raise on every later read of the session (a 500). A per-item `notice_parties` would skip `store_notice_parties` and store no ids. So the base serializer carries `lien`, `claim_status` and `notice_parties` read-only (a sent value is ignored), and `NestedDebtInfoSerializer` is the only writer. No frontend code calls the per-item paths.
  - **`AddressAnswersMixin(serializers.Serializer)`**, the one address declaration for debts (here), contracts and codebtors (Task 3 imports it and defines none of its own):
    - Fields, each `required=False`: `street_address` `CharField(max_length=255, allow_blank=True)`, `street_2` `CharField(max_length=255, allow_blank=True)`, `city` `CharField(max_length=100, allow_blank=True)`, `state` `CharField(max_length=2, allow_blank=True)`, `zip_code` `CharField(max_length=10, allow_blank=True)`, `outside_us` `BooleanField()`. These are Task 1's column widths.
    - `validate_state(self, value)` returns `value.strip().upper()`, with up to two characters and blank allowed. No letter test (R7): `"il"` saves as `"IL"`; `"I"` saves as `"I"`; `"ILL"` is a 400. The two-letter test is Complete's (Task 6), so one half-typed row never refuses the whole list.
  - **`_save_rows_by_id(session, model, rows, scoped=None) -> list`**:
    - `scoped` is a dict `{"<attname>": <Model>}`, for example `{"secured_by_id": AssetInfo}` for debts. Task 3 passes `{"vehicle_asset_id": AssetInfo, "owed_debt_id": DebtInfo}` for contracts.
    - Before the loop it reads, once per call, each scoped model's ids on this session (`Model.objects.filter(session=session).values_list("pk", flat=True)`), after any rows saved earlier in the same request, so a debt that links to an asset sent in the same PATCH sees that PATCH's deletes.
    - For each row: when the attname is a key of `data`, a value that is not one of those ids (an id of another session or another filer, an id no row has, or a removed one) is stored as `None`. A key the row didn't send is left alone, so a partial row keeps its link. A `None` stays `None`. The reply is built from what was stored, so a foreign id is never echoed (B7).
    - It also stores the answers a model keeps as JSON, through one helper `_assign(row, data)` used for both a create (`model(session=session)`, then `_assign`, then `save()`) and an update. **`_assign` is the only place a JSON column is encoded:** each key of `getattr(model, "JSON_ANSWERS", ())` is `json.dumps(value)` (a `None` stays `None`), a `notice_parties` key goes to `row.store_notice_parties(value)`, and every other key is `setattr`. Task 3 relies on this for `Codebtor.links` (`JSON_ANSWERS = ("links",)`): its row serializer hands over a Python list of `{kind, id}` dicts, already filtered to live rows, and never dumps it. No serializer's `validate` calls `json.dumps`.
    - The delete of stored rows the list left out is unchanged; the session lock is the caller's.
  - **Dead link on read (R6).** "Dead" means null, or a row now on another session.
    - A null link reads as null. A link whose row is now on another session reads as null. `DebtInfoSerializer.to_representation` sets `data["secured_by"] = None` when `instance.secured_by_id` is set and `instance.secured_by.session_id != instance.session_id`.
    - So the session read doesn't query once per debt, `IntakeSessionSerializer.to_representation` reads `ordered_debts(instance.debts.select_related("secured_by"))`, and `update` returns rows `_save_rows_by_id` saved (it sets `secured_by_id` only, so the check there falls back to one query per row with a link; acceptable, at most one per row).
    - `AssetViewSet.update` can move a row to another of the filer's sessions after the link was made, and it doesn't touch debts; that is why the check is on read and not only on save.
  - **`account_last_4(number) -> str`** in `claims.py`: the last four digits of the digits in `number` (fewer if it has fewer), `""` for `None` or none. Pure, no model import.

### Task 3: Contract and codebtor rows save every answer

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/serializers.py`: `ExecutoryContractSerializer`, `CodebtorSerializer`, `ExecutoryContractRowSerializer` and `CodebtorRowSerializer` (now at `:1151-1196`).
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/views.py`:
  - `_save_list` (`:53-68`) gains `scoped=None` and passes `context={"session": session}` to the row serializer.
  - `contracts` (`:506-523`) and `codebtors` (`:542-557`): the PUT passes `scoped`, and the single `POST` takes the lock before it validates (R22).
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_contract_codebtor_answers.py`
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_list_saves.py:194-217` and `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_session_lock.py:651-682`. The `validate` spies wrap the real method (R22), and the POST cases join both parametrizations.
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_list_saves.py:118-141` (`test_a_list_with_one_bad_row_saves_nothing[contracts-bad_row0]`). Its bad contract row at `:116`, `{"counterparty_name": "", "contract_type": "lease"}`, now saves (a blank name is allowed), so the PUT is 200. The row becomes `{"counterparty_name": "Metro Gym", "contract_type": "boat"}`, which is still a 400. Change the setup only; the all-or-nothing assertion stays.

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's columns (`street_2`, `outside_us`, `lease_kind`, `monthly_payment`, `vehicle_asset`, `owes_now`, `owed_debt`, `Codebtor.links` with `get_links()`, `LEASE_KINDS`, `LINK_KINDS`), and Task 2's `_save_rows_by_id(session, model, rows, scoped=None)` (R6) and `AddressAnswersMixin`. An id in `scoped` that names no row of that model on this session saves as `None`.
- Produces:
  - **Contract list row:**
    - Address: from Task 2's `AddressAnswersMixin` (`street_address` 255, `street_2` 255, `city` 100, `state` 2 upper-cased, `zip_code` 10, `outside_us`); the contract serializers list the six names in `Meta.fields` and declare none.
    - `counterparty_name` and `contract_type` may be blank. `description` as today.
    - `lease_kind` (`LEASE_KINDS`, null allowed), `monthly_payment` (`DecimalField(10, 2, min_value=0, required=False, allow_null=True)`, declared, since DRF would map the encrypted field to a string), `owes_now` (`yes`/`no`/null).
    - `vehicle_asset` and `owed_debt`: `IntegerField(source="<name>_id", required=False, allow_null=True)`.
  - **Codebtor list row:** `street_2`, `outside_us`, `relationship` (blank allowed), `links` (`[{kind, id}]`, live entries only, no duplicates).
  - **Base serializers** (the single `POST`, and the `GET` the wizard reads): carry the same answers, with `vehicle_asset`, `owed_debt` and `links` read-only (R6). `counterparty_name`, `name` and the contract's `contract_type` stay required. A codebtor's `relationship` stays optional.
  - **`contract_type` is declared, not left to the model.** Task 1 gave the column `blank=True`, so DRF would map it to an optional `ChoiceField`, and the single `POST` with no type would be a 201. The base `ExecutoryContractSerializer` declares `contract_type = serializers.ChoiceField(choices=ExecutoryContract.CONTRACT_TYPE_CHOICES)` (required). `ExecutoryContractRowSerializer` redeclares it `required=False, allow_blank=True`. `Codebtor.relationship` is already declared on the base `CodebtorSerializer`, so only `CodebtorRowSerializer` adds `allow_blank=True`.
  - **Reads** (both serializers, in `to_representation`): "dead" means null, or a row now on another session (R6). A dead FK (`vehicle_asset`, `owed_debt`) reads as `None`, as Task 2's debt rows do, and a codebtor link to a debt or contract that is gone or on another session is dropped from the list. The stored text isn't rewritten.
- **Decisions this task makes** (no ruling covers them):
  - The live check for `links` runs in `CodebtorRowSerializer.validate`, reading the session's debt and contract ids through `self.context["session"]`. That is the stored-row read R22 hands over, so the spies now matter. The check can't live in `_save_rows_by_id`'s `scoped`, which handles only FK columns.
  - `validate` returns `links` as a **Python list** of `{kind, id}` dicts, filtered to the session's live debts and contracts, and never dumps it. `_save_rows_by_id`'s `_assign` (Task 2) is the only place JSON columns are encoded: it `json.dumps` the `Codebtor.JSON_ANSWERS` key `links`.
  - The address declarations are Task 2's `AddressAnswersMixin`: import it, and don't define a second one. The contract and codebtor serializers (base and row) inherit it, as `class ExecutoryContractSerializer(AddressAnswersMixin, serializers.ModelSerializer)`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the serializers.**
  - The base serializers get the new fields as above, `to_representation` readings of dead links, and read-only links.
  - The base `ExecutoryContractSerializer` declares `contract_type` as a required `ChoiceField(choices=ExecutoryContract.CONTRACT_TYPE_CHOICES)`; the row serializers (`id` writable) relax the name and type with `required=False, allow_blank=True` (`contract_type` redeclared; `CodebtorRowSerializer.relationship` adds `allow_blank=True`) and make the links writable.
  - `CodebtorRowSerializer.validate(attrs)`:
    - Reads the session's debt ids and contract ids with two `values_list("pk", flat=True)` queries.

    - Sets `attrs["links"]` to that list (a Python list, not a string).
    - Does nothing when `links` wasn't sent.

```python
with transaction.atomic():
    lock_session(session.pk)
    serializer = ExecutoryContractSerializer(data=request.data)
    serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
    serializer.save(session=session)
    session.reopen()
```

### Task 4: Claim answers on the session, and drafts confirm on Continue

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/serializers.py`:
  - Add `ClaimAnswersField` (after `AssetLinesField`, `:773`) and `_save_claim_answers` (after `_save_asset_lines`, `:781`).
  - `IntakeSessionSerializer` (`:789`) gains the `claim_answers` field, its `validate_claim_answers`, a place in `Meta.fields`, and a line in `create` and `update`.
  - `_INTAKE_DATA` (`:761`) gains `"claim_answers"`, so a save reopens a completed intake.
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/views.py`:
  - `update_step` (`:207`) confirms the drafts at step 7.
  - The `partial_update` override (`:183-197`) is deleted. `UpdateModelMixin.partial_update` then calls the locked `update` (`:172`).
  - `get_queryset` (`:134`) adds `"claim_answers"` to `select_related` (R11).
  - If `DebtInfo` is then unused in `views.py`, ruff names it. Remove the import only if so.
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_claim_answers.py`
- Modify (moved pins, §7.7):
  - `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_views.py:33-53` (`test_debts_patch_clears_is_draft`).
  - `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_session_nested_save.py:280-304`.
  - `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_completion.py:750-789`.
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_session_lock.py` (`WRITES`, after `"Complete"` at `:540`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's `ClaimAnswers` (`get_answers()`, `merge(sent)`, `KEYS`, `TEXT_LIMITS`, `related_name="claim_answers"`).
- Produces:
  - A session PATCH or PUT may carry `claim_answers`. Only the keys sent change, a null clears one, and an unknown key or a value of the wrong shape is a 400. The reply's `claim_answers` is the stored dict, or `{}` with no row.
  - **The keys and their shapes:**
    - `no_debts`: `true` or null. `false` is a 400, since "unselected" is null (B35).
    - `contracts_answer`, `codebtors_answer`, `community_lived`, `community_spouse_lived_with`: `"yes"`, `"no"` or null.
    - Text, or null: `community_state` (100), `community_spouse_name` (255), `community_spouse_street_address` (255), `community_spouse_street_2` (255), `community_spouse_city` (100), `community_spouse_state` (2, stored `.strip().upper()`, R7), `community_spouse_zip_code` (10).
    - `community_spouse_outside_us`: a boolean, or null.
  - **A 400 names the key and never the value**, since a value may be a person's name or address. The message is `Not a claim answer: <key>.` for an unknown key and `Check the answer for <key>.` for a bad value.
  - `update_step` with `current_step == 7` leaves no draft debt on the session, and its reply shows them confirmed.
- **Decisions this task makes** (no ruling covers them):
  - `"false"`-like values for `no_debts` are refused, as above.
  - A text key's length limits are `ClaimAnswers.TEXT_LIMITS`, which Task 1 defines beside `KEYS` (the limits above). This task reads it and adds no limits of its own.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the confirmation.**
  - Delete the `partial_update` override in `views.py`.
  - In `update_step`, right after `session.save()` and BEFORE the `if "data" in request.data:` block, add one statement for step 7:

```python
if current_step == 7:
    DebtInfo.objects.filter(session=session, is_draft=True).update(is_draft=False)
```

  - It goes before the `data` block, not after it. A save carrying `data.debts` sets `session.sent_rows` to the `DebtInfo` objects it loaded, and the reply builds `debts` from them. Cleared after the save, those in-memory rows would still read `is_draft` True although the stored rows are confirmed. Cleared first, the rows the save loads are already confirmed, and a row the save creates is never a draft.
  - `session` comes from `lock_session`, so it carries no prefetched debts and the reply's `ordered_debts(instance.debts.all())` reads the confirmed rows. The method is already `@transaction.atomic` and locked.

    );

    expect(screen.queryByText(LIVES_HERE_QUESTION)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps the answer in the row, and shows it again after a reload', () => {
    const first = render(<Walk part={1} rows={[placeRow()]} answers={{ '1': 'yes' }} />);
    fireEvent.click(livesHere('1')[0]);
    expect(latest.rows[0].details.lives_here).toBe('yes');
    first.unmount();

    render(
      <Walk
        part={1}
        rows={[placeRow({ details: { ...placeRow().details, lives_here: 'no' } })]}
        answers={{ '1': 'yes' }}
      />
    );
    expect(livesHere('1').find((button) => button.checked)?.value).toBe('no');
  });

  it('leaves Continue on Part 1 as it was', () => {
    // lives_here is no Assets gap (R24): Task 14 names it only once a debt links to the row.
    render(<Walk part={1} rows={[placeRow()]} answers={{ '1': 'yes' }} />);
    expect(latest.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(partIsComplete(1, [placeRow()], { '1': 'yes' })).toBe(true);
  });
  ```

  In `upl-assets-copy.test.ts`, `ASSET_SENTENCES` gains `LIVES_HERE_QUESTION: copy.LIVES_HERE_QUESTION,` after `WHO_WITH_SOMEONE`.

### Task 6: The claim gaps: one table, one function, the sentences

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/claims.py`:
  - Add `ClaimStepGap`, `claim_step_gaps(session)`, `claim_row_name(name, nameless)`, `address_missing(street, city, state, zip_code, outside_us)` and `link_is_live(linked, session)` (R6, R8–R10). Called by nothing yet.
  - Change the docstring's "Pure: no models." to say the module imports no models and reads a session's relations by attribute (R10).
  - The sentences are module constants spelled like `copy.ts`'s (`DEBT_GAP_AMOUNT = "Enter the amount you owe {name}. It must be above $0."`), filled with `.format(name=…)`.
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/builders.py`:
  - New `complete_debt(session, **over) -> DebtInfo`, which also clears `no_debts` on its session's `ClaimAnswers`.
  - New `answer_claims(session, **answers) -> ClaimAnswers`.
  - New `complete_contract(session, **over) -> ExecutoryContract` and `complete_codebtor(session, links=None, **over) -> Codebtor`, beside `complete_debt` and `answer_claims`. `test_claim_gaps.py` and Task 14's `test_session_lock.py` use them.
  - All four builders read and write the session's `ClaimAnswers` through `getattr(session, "claim_answers", None)`, as `answer_household` does for its rows: when the row is missing, they create it and set `session.claim_answers` to it. They never re-read it with a fresh query, so the held session's copy is never stale.
- Modify: `frontend/src/constants/copy.ts` (every gap sentence in the table, as one-literal constants)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_claim_gaps.py`:
  - One test per row of the table.
  - `test_continue_and_complete_give_the_same_sentence`, which pins each constant to the server's sentence, as `test_asset_gaps.py:123-140` does.
- Create: `frontend/src/test/upl-claims-copy.test.ts` (R13), listing every constant, and an empty `OTHER_CLAIM_SENTENCES: Record<string, string>` that Tasks 8–11 fill with their `copy.ts` sentences.

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Tasks 1–5's fields.
- Produces:
  - `ClaimStepGap(message: str, step: str)`.
  - `claim_step_gaps(session) -> list[ClaimStepGap]`, in the table's order.
  - `claim_row_name`, `address_missing` and `link_is_live`, which Task 14 and the steps' copies mirror.
    - **`claim_row_name` takes the row's name text, not the row** (`claim_row_name(name, nameless)`), since the three rows keep it in `creditor_name`, `counterparty_name` and `name`. `claimRowName(name, nameless)` in `claimNames.ts` does the same.
    - `link_is_live(linked, session)` takes the row a link names, or None: `linked is not None and linked.session_id == session.pk`. A codebtor's `links` hold ids, so they are checked inline against the ids of the session's own debts and contracts.
  - `complete_debt(session, **over)`: a confirmed, unsecured `credit_card` debt with every answer: "Demo Card Services", $500.00, a Demo address, status `[]`, offset `"no"`, notice `[]`. Overridable.
  - `answer_claims(session, *, no_debts=None, contracts_answer="no", codebtors_answer="no", community_lived="no", **more)`.
  - `complete_contract(session, **over) -> ExecutoryContract`: "Demo Property Management LLC", a Demo address, `contract_type="lease"`, `lease_kind="residential"`, `description="Apartment lease"`, `owes_now="no"`. Overridable. It also sets `contracts_answer="yes"` on the session's `ClaimAnswers`.
  - `complete_codebtor(session, links=None, **over) -> Codebtor`: "Demo Codebtor", a Demo address, and `links` defaulting to `[{"kind": "debt", "id": <the session's first debt's pk>}]` (the caller builds the debt first, with `complete_debt`). It also sets `codebtors_answer="yes"`.
  - The `copy.ts` constants below, which Tasks 8–10 import.
- **The gap table.** It is the one source for the server and the steps' copies.
  - `{name}` is `claim_row_name`: the row's name stripped, else `NAMELESS_DEBT`, `NAMELESS_CONTRACT` or `NAMELESS_CODEBTOR`.
  - An address is missing (`address_missing`) when the street or city is blank, or, unless `outside_us`, the state isn't two letters or the ZIP is blank. Ticked outside the US, the sentence is the street-and-city form.
  - A link is live (`link_is_live`) when it names a row on the same session.
  - The rows below are the table. Each gives the order (step), the condition and the constant.
  - **Assets** (`step="assets"`), first:
    - Condition: a debt linked by a live link to a line 1 or line 4 row whose `lives_here` is null, one gap per such row.
    - Sentence, server only: `Answer “Do you live here?” for {row} on line {line} of the Assets step.`, where `{row}` is `_row_name`.
  - **Debts** (`step="debts"`): the step-level gaps, then each row in `ordered_debts` order with its gaps in this order.
    - D0. A draft remains: the existing `Check the debts read from your documents on the Amounts Owed step.` Server only, never on the step's copy (§7.2).
    - D1. No debt rows and `no_debts` isn't true: `DEBT_GAP_NO_ANSWER`.
    - D2. `no_debts` true with rows: `DEBT_GAP_NONE_BUT_LISTED`.
    - D3. Name blank: `DEBT_GAP_NO_NAME`.
    - D4. `debt_type` blank: `CLAIM_GAP_KIND`.
    - D5. Amount not above $0: `DEBT_GAP_AMOUNT`, or `DEBT_GAP_AMOUNT_NO_NAME` for a nameless row.
    - D6. Address missing:
      - Not outside the US: `CLAIM_GAP_ADDRESS`, or `DEBT_GAP_ADDRESS_NO_NAME` for a nameless row.
      - Outside the US: `CLAIM_GAP_STREET_CITY`, or `DEBT_GAP_STREET_CITY_NO_NAME` for a nameless row.
    - D7. Secured, and no live `secured_by`: `DEBT_GAP_PROPERTY`.
    - D8. Secured, and `collateral_description` blank: `DEBT_GAP_DESCRIBE`.
    - D9. Secured, and `lien` null or no kind: `DEBT_GAP_LIEN`.
    - D10. Secured, with kind `other` and blank text: `DEBT_GAP_LIEN_OTHER`.
    - D11. Secured, and `monthly_payment` null: `DEBT_GAP_PAYMENT`.
    - D12. Secured, linked by a live link to a row whose `lives_here` is `"yes"`, and `home_payment_line` null: `DEBT_GAP_HOME_LINE`.
    - D13. Secured, linked by a live link to a row whose shape asks no `who` (R25), and `collateral_who` null: `DEBT_GAP_WHO`. A row with no line (an old row) counts as a shape that asks no `who`, so D13 applies to it. The steps' copies mirror this: `asksWho(undefined)` is false.
    - D14. `claim_status` null: `DEBT_GAP_STATUS`.
    - D15. Not secured, and `offset_answer` null: `DEBT_GAP_OFFSET`.
    - D16. `notice_parties` null: `DEBT_GAP_NOTICE`.
    - D17. A notice party with a blank name or a missing address: `DEBT_GAP_NOTICE_PARTY`, once per debt.
  - **Contracts** (`step="contracts"`): the step-level gaps, then each row in id order.
    - C1. `contracts_answer` null: `CONTRACT_GAP_NO_ANSWER`.
    - C2. `"yes"` with no row: `CONTRACT_GAP_YES_BUT_NONE`.
    - C3. `"no"` with rows: `CONTRACT_GAP_NO_BUT_LISTED`.
    - C4. Name blank: `CONTRACT_GAP_NO_NAME`.
    - C5. Address missing: `CLAIM_GAP_ADDRESS` or `CLAIM_GAP_STREET_CITY`.
    - C6. `contract_type` blank: `CLAIM_GAP_KIND`.
    - C7. `description` blank: `CONTRACT_GAP_PURPOSE`.
    - C8. A lease with `lease_kind` null: `CONTRACT_GAP_LEASE_KIND`.
    - C9. A vehicle lease with no live `vehicle_asset`: `CONTRACT_GAP_VEHICLE`.
    - C10. A vehicle lease with `monthly_payment` null: `CONTRACT_GAP_PAYMENT`.
    - C11. `owes_now` null: `CONTRACT_GAP_OWES_NOW`.
    - C12. `"yes"` with no live `owed_debt`: `CONTRACT_GAP_OWED_DEBT`.
    - C13. B44: a vehicle lease whose live `owed_debt` is a secured (`is_secured`) debt whose live `secured_by` is the same row as the lease's live `vehicle_asset`, with both payments above $0: `CONTRACT_GAP_SAME_VEHICLE`, where `{debt}` is the debt's `claim_row_name`. The steps' copies mirror this, `is_secured` included.
  - **Codebtors** (`step="codebtors"`): the step-level gaps, then each row in id order.
    - H1. `codebtors_answer` null: `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_ANSWER`.
    - H2. `"yes"` with no row: `CODEBTOR_GAP_YES_BUT_NONE`.
    - H3. `"no"` with rows: `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_BUT_LISTED`.
    - H4. `community_lived` null: `CODEBTOR_GAP_COMMUNITY`.
    - H5. `community_lived` `"yes"` and `community_spouse_lived_with` null: `CODEBTOR_GAP_SPOUSE_LIVED`.
    - H6. Both `"yes"`, and `community_state` or `community_spouse_name` is blank, or the spouse's address is missing: `CODEBTOR_GAP_SPOUSE_DETAILS`.
    - H7. Name blank: `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_NAME`.
    - H8. Address missing: `CLAIM_GAP_ADDRESS` or `CLAIM_GAP_STREET_CITY`.
    - H9. No live link: `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_LINK`.
- **The sentences** (`copy.ts`):
  - **Names:** `NAMELESS_DEBT = 'one amount owed'`, `NAMELESS_CONTRACT = 'one contract'` and `NAMELESS_CODEBTOR = 'one codebtor'`.
  - **Shared:**
    - `CLAIM_GAP_KIND = 'Choose a kind for {name}.'`
    - `CLAIM_GAP_ADDRESS = 'Enter the street, city, two-letter state and ZIP for {name}.'` (§7.2)
    - `CLAIM_GAP_STREET_CITY = 'Enter the street and city for {name}.'`
  - **Debts:**
    - `DEBT_GAP_NO_ANSWER = 'List each amount you owe, or press “I have no amounts owed.”'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_NONE_BUT_LISTED = 'You said you have no amounts owed, but some are listed. Remove them, or change that answer.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_NO_NAME = 'One amount owed has no creditor name. Enter who you owe.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_AMOUNT = 'Enter the amount you owe {name}. It must be above $0.'` (§2)
    - `DEBT_GAP_AMOUNT_NO_NAME = 'Enter the amount you owe on the row with no creditor name. It must be above $0.'` (§2)
    - `DEBT_GAP_ADDRESS_NO_NAME = 'Enter the street, city, two-letter state and ZIP on the row with no creditor name.'` (§7.2)
    - `DEBT_GAP_STREET_CITY_NO_NAME = 'Enter the street and city on the row with no creditor name.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_PROPERTY = 'Choose the property that secures {name}.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_DESCRIBE = 'Describe the property that secures {name}.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_LIEN = 'Check at least one kind of lien for {name}.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_LIEN_OTHER = 'Say what the other kind of lien is, for {name}.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_PAYMENT = 'Enter the monthly payment for {name}. Enter 0 if you pay nothing on it.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_HOME_LINE = 'Say whether {name} is a first mortgage or an additional one, such as home equity.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_WHO = 'Answer who owns the property that secures {name}.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_STATUS = 'Check any that fit {name}: contingent, unliquidated or disputed. Or choose None of these.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_OFFSET = 'Answer whether {name} is subject to offset.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_NOTICE = 'Answer whether anyone else is contacting you about {name}.'`
    - `DEBT_GAP_NOTICE_PARTY = 'Enter the name and full address of each person contacting you about {name}.'`
  - **Contracts:**
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_NO_ANSWER = 'Answer whether you have any contracts or leases.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_YES_BUT_NONE = 'You answered Yes. Add each contract or lease, or change the answer to No.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_NO_BUT_LISTED = 'You answered No, but contracts or leases are listed. Remove them, or change the answer to Yes.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_NO_NAME = "One contract has no name. Enter the other party's name."`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_PURPOSE = 'Enter what the contract or lease is for, for {name}.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_LEASE_KIND = 'Choose the kind of lease for {name}.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_VEHICLE = 'Choose the vehicle you lease from {name}.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_PAYMENT = 'Enter the monthly lease payment for {name}.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_OWES_NOW = 'Answer “Do you owe money on this now?” for {name}.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_OWED_DEBT = 'Choose the amount owed that goes with {name}, or add it on the Amounts Owed step.'`
    - `CONTRACT_GAP_SAME_VEHICLE = 'Your lease with {name} and your debt to {debt} are on the same vehicle, and each has a monthly payment above $0. Enter the monthly payment once, on either row, and $0 on the other.'` (§7.3)
  - **Codebtors:**
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_ANSWER = 'Answer whether you have any codebtors.'`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_YES_BUT_NONE = 'You answered Yes. Add each codebtor, or change the answer to No.'`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_BUT_LISTED = 'You answered No, but codebtors are listed. Remove them, or change the answer to Yes.'`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_COMMUNITY = 'Did you live in a community property state or territory in the last 8 years? Answer Yes or No.'`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_SPOUSE_LIVED = 'Answer whether your spouse, former spouse or legal equivalent lived with you then.'`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_SPOUSE_DETAILS = 'Enter the community property state, and that person’s name and full address.'`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_NAME = "One codebtor has no name. Enter the person's name."`
    - `CODEBTOR_GAP_NO_LINK = 'Choose which amounts owed or contracts {name} shares with you.'`
- **Two sentences are server only and exempt from the UPL list.** The Assets entry's `Answer “Do you live here?” for {row} on line {line} of the Assets step.` and D0's `Check the debts read from your documents on the Amounts Owed step.` have no `copy.ts` constant, since the wizard's steps never say them (Continue on Assets doesn't ask `lives_here`, and D0 is not on the step's copy). `upl-claims-copy.test.ts` reads `copy.ts` only, so it cannot score them. Both hold no advice word and no figure.
- **Reading level.** The skeleton's wording of `DEBT_GAP_STATUS` and `CODEBTOR_GAP_COMMUNITY` scored over `reading-level.test.ts`'s ceiling of grade 9 (10.4, 10.7). The table above holds their replacements (5.7, 5.6), ruled in R12.

**What the implementer decides, because the table doesn't:**

- `claim_step_gaps` reads `session.debts.all()` (drafts included, so a draft's gaps show), `session.executory_contracts.all()`, `session.codebtors.all()` and `getattr(session, "claim_answers", None)`. No row means every answer is null. Debts are `ordered_debts(rows)`, the other two by `pk`.
- "Secured" is `is_secured`. A debt's `lien`, `claim_status` and `notice_parties` come from `get_lien()`, `get_claim_status()` and `get_notice_parties()` (null stays null).
- D12 and D13 read the row only through a live link (`link_is_live`), as D7 does. The linked row's `lives_here` is `linked.get_details().get("lives_here")`. A linked row whose `ab_line` is not in `LINE_BY_KEY` (an old row) counts as a shape that asks no `who`, so D13 applies to it: the Assets step has its own gap for that row.
- The Assets entry looks at the live-linked rows of the session's debts only, drafts included, one gap per row, in `ordered_assets` order, naming the row with `asset_lines._row_name` and its line.
- A row's gaps all come before the next row's (R8). A name-blank gap (D3, C4, H7) comes with the other gaps of that row, not instead of them.
- A `monthly_payment` of None counts as 0 for C13.
- Nothing in this module logs, and no exception message is built from anything but the table's sentences (Global Constraints: logs and errors).

### Task 7: The wizard holds and saves every claim answer

**Files:**

- Create: `frontend/src/utils/claimRows.ts` (the three blank tests, the list filter, `sentDebt`, `noDebtsToSend`; the pattern of `utils/householdRows.ts`)
- Create: `frontend/src/utils/__tests__/claimRows.test.ts`
- Modify: `frontend/src/constants/copy.ts` (`CHOOSE_ONE`)
- Modify: `frontend/src/pages/IntakeWizard.tsx`:
  - `claimAnswers` state, hydrated from the session read (`:279-316`). `_securedPressed` and the contracts' payment are hydrated there too.
  - The step 6, 7 and 8 bodies (`:423-446`) and the three steps' props (`:805-830`).
  - `StepBody` (`:104`) and `withoutKey` (`:110`), widened to every `_` key (R15).
  - The contracts and codebtors branches of `saveCurrentStepData` (`:605-626`) send two requests (R16). The session branch carries `claim_answers` unchanged.
  - `useAutoSave`'s `enabled` (`:480`) also covers `debts`, `contracts` and `codebtors` (B4, B47).
- Modify: `frontend/src/components/wizard/steps/DebtsStep.tsx`, `ContractsStep.tsx`, `CodebtorsStep.tsx`:
  - The prop types, widened to the new props. The steps **do not destructure them**: `noUnusedParameters` refuses an unused binding. Tasks 8–10 start using them.
  - **One more change, which the skeleton didn't list** (see "Decisions"): `ContractsStep`'s and `CodebtorsStep`'s empty row loses its pre-selected `'contract'` and `'other'` (B34), and each of their two selects gets a blank first option, `CHOOSE_ONE`.
- Modify: `frontend/src/pages/__tests__/IntakeWizard.test.tsx` (the helpers, the shared fixtures `storedDebt`, `storedLease` and `storedCodebtor`, the pins listed under Step 1, and the new tests)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Tasks 2–4's API shapes (the nested debt row, the list PUTs, `claim_answers` on a session PATCH).
- Produces, for Tasks 8–10:
  - **The steps' props.**
    - `DebtsStep`: `{rows: DebtRow[], onRowsChange: (rows: DebtRow[]) => void, noDebts: boolean, onNoDebtsChange: (pressed: boolean) => void, assets: WizardRow<AssetInfo>[], onValidationChange}`.
    - `ContractsStep`: `{rows: ContractRow[], onRowsChange, answer: YesNo | null, onAnswerChange: (answer: YesNo) => void, debts: DebtRow[], assets: WizardRow<AssetInfo>[], onValidationChange}`. Task 9 adds `onGoToStep`.
    - `CodebtorsStep`: `{rows: CodebtorRow[], onRowsChange, answers: ClaimAnswers, onAnswersChange: (answers: ClaimAnswers) => void, debts: DebtRow[], contracts: ContractRow[], onValidationChange}`. A step passes `onAnswersChange` the whole object, merged.
  - **`utils/claimRows.ts`:**
    - `isBlankDebt(row: DebtRow): boolean`, `isBlankContract(row: ContractRow): boolean` and `isBlankCodebtor(row: CodebtorRow): boolean`. A row is blank when every answer on it is empty, and a row's id isn't one of them.
    - `keptRows<T extends {id?: number}>(rows: T[], isBlank: (row: T) => boolean): T[]`: the rows a save carries, a row with an id or one that isn't blank. **The steps' gap functions filter with it too**, so a copy of a gap never sees a row the server never gets.
    - `sentDebt(row: DebtRow): DebtRow` and `noDebtsToSend(kept: unknown[], pressed: boolean): true | null`.
  - **Wizard state:** `claimAnswers: ClaimAnswers`. `noDebts` is `claimAnswers.no_debts === true`.
  - **A loaded debt row** carries `_securedPressed: !debt.is_draft` (R15, B43). A new row gets none, which reads as unpressed. **Task 8 owns every other write of the mark** (its rule is in Task 8).
- **Decisions this task makes** (no ruling covers them; each names its cost):
  - **Blank test, per row.** Text counts after a trim. A box that holds `null`, `undefined`, `''`, `false` or an amount of `undefined` is empty.
    - `amount_owed` of 0 is empty too (a stored 0 means "not given yet", B29).
    - `monthly_payment` of 0 is an answer (B18).
    - `claim_status` and `notice_parties` of `[]` are answers ("None of these", "No").
    - `lien` is empty when it is null or has no kind.
    - `is_secured` true and `outside_us` true are answers, and `_securedPressed` is not (it is no stored answer).
    - Cost if wrong: a row with only a kind picked is kept or dropped differently from a filer's expectation. Nothing is lost, since the pick is one click.
  - **Rows go as held.** A body row is the wizard's row with its `_key` (pairing needs it) and, for a debt, `amount_owed` as 0 when blank and `monthly_payment` as null when blank (R14, as today's body does for the payment).
    - No defaults are added for the new keys: a loaded row always has them all, since the serializer returns every field, and Tasks 8–10 build every new row with all of them. A key a row lacks is left out of the request, and the server keeps what it holds.
    - Cost if wrong: a row built without a key keeps the server's value instead of clearing it.
  - **Contracts' payment.** `hydrateContract` turns a stored `monthly_payment` into a number and keeps null as null (`decimalToNumber(...) ?? null`), only when the row has the key. The body sends it as held, so Task 9's empty box (null) clears the figure.
  - **The second request skips when overtaken.** If a later save of the list has gone out while this PUT was on its way, this save returns without its PATCH: the later save carries the same answers. Cost if wrong: an answer typed between the two saves reaches the server one save later.
  - **The PUT's ids go into the wizard's rows before the PATCH is sent.** A PATCH that then fails leaves the rows holding the ids the server gave them, so the next save updates those rows and never creates them again (Review Focus 2).
  - **Notice parties** (R27: ids are not paired from the reply). A party in Task 8's nested rows carries no `_key`, and the wizard doesn't pair its id from the reply. An autosave sends a new party without an id, and the server gives it a fresh one each time, so no id is reused and nothing is lost; its id changes on each save of its debt until the session is read again. Nothing else refers to a party's id. Cost: ids burn faster, and `next_notice_party_id` grows; the 10-party cap bounds the list, not the counter.
  - **`CHOOSE_ONE`** is added here, not in Task 8, because this task's two selects need it first. Task 8 imports it.
  - **Not carried over from the skeleton:** the validity pins `:3604` and `:3619` do **not** move in this task. The steps' own name rules stay until Tasks 9 and 10 replace them with the gap sentences, so both tests still pass at the end of Task 7. The pin that does move is `:3294` (see Step 1).

**Wizard bodies**, exactly (R16, R18; keys in the order shown):

```ts
// step 6: kept = keptRows(debtsData, isBlankDebt).map(sentDebt)
{ debts: kept, claim_answers: { no_debts: noDebtsToSend(kept, noDebts) } }
// step 7
{ contracts: keptRows(contractsData, isBlankContract), claim_answers: { contracts_answer: claimAnswers.contracts_answer ?? null } }
// step 8: every key below as `claimAnswers[key] ?? null`
{ codebtors: keptRows(codebtorsData, isBlankCodebtor), claim_answers: {
    codebtors_answer, community_lived, community_spouse_lived_with, community_state,
    community_spouse_name, community_spouse_street_address, community_spouse_street_2,
    community_spouse_city, community_spouse_state, community_spouse_zip_code,
    community_spouse_outside_us } }
```

A text key sent as null clears it, which is the same as blank. Step 6's `no_debts` is `true` or null, never `false` (Task 4 refuses it).

**Hydration of `noDebts`:** the session read sets `claimAnswers` to `fresh.claim_answers ?? {}` with `no_debts` set to `true` only when the same read holds no debt row, and null otherwise. So a stored `true` beside rows (which only an old client or a direct call can leave) never shows a pressed button the filer can't unpress. Cost if wrong: Complete still names that case (Task 6's D2); the step doesn't.

    ? Object.entries(value).some(([key, inner]) => key.startsWith('_') || hasBrowserKey(inner))
    : false;

Each input's `name` is `${prefix}${field}${index === undefined ? '' : '_' + index}` and its id `field-<name>`. Debts use prefix `''` with an index, a notice party `notice_<i>_<j>_` with none, a codebtor `codebtor_` with an index, H line 2's spouse `community_spouse_` with none.

**The pressed mark (B42, B43), exactly.** It is `_securedPressed` on a `DebtRow`, browser-only, never sent (Task 7's `sentDebt` drops it, and `withoutKey` strips it).

- **Set to true in two places only:** at hydration for a loaded row that isn't a draft (Task 7), and in `answerSecured`, when the filer changes the `is_secured_<i>` radio.
- **Never cleared.** A new row is built with `_securedPressed: false`. A scanned draft is loaded with `false`. Nothing else writes the mark, so no id, save or reply can turn a new row into a pressed one: do not derive it from `id !== undefined`, since a new row gets an id from its first autosave.
- **Read in one place,** `changeType`: the type's `is_secured` goes onto the row only while `_securedPressed !== true`. `priority_classification` follows the type either way, as it does today.
- **Never written on mount.** The step makes no `onRowsChange` call in an effect, or a visit alone would change the body after the loaded copy was taken and save.
- **The radios' display:** Yes when `is_secured` is true; No when it is false and (`_securedPressed` is true or `debt_type` is chosen); otherwise nothing. So a new row with no kind has nothing checked, and picking a kind pre-fills the answer (U:77, the one exception), which the filer can change.

**Decisions this task makes** (no ruling covers them; each names its cost if wrong):

- **D or E/F words follow the secured answer:** the date and the community tick read as D's on a secured row and as E/F's otherwise. Cost: a row with no answer yet shows E/F's.
- **A secured answer that changes clears what its side hides.** Pressing or pre-filling No sets `secured_by`, `home_payment_line` and `collateral_who` to null; Yes sets `offset_answer` to null. `collateral_description` and `lien` stay. A pick of another property clears `home_payment_line` and `collateral_who`, since both depend on the row picked. Cost: a mistaken press loses a link.
- **Unticking the last status or the last lien kind** sets the answer to null (unanswered), never `[]`: `[]` is "None of these". Unticking "Other" clears `lien.other`.
- **Notice:** Yes with no party yet adds one empty party; No sets `[]`; the filer removing the last party leaves `[]`. Parties are keyed by position in the DOM (they have no `_key`, Task 7). "Add another" is disabled at `MAX_NOTICE_PARTIES` (10, R5).
- **The status sentence always shows** while a gap exists, including the first one on an untouched step (D1, "List each amount you owe, or press…"). Continue waiting with no word said is the anti-pattern the trauma-informed guide names. Cost: the sentence shows before the filer has begun.
- **Checkbox groups** (status, lien) carry the required mark on their legend and no `aria-required` on their inputs; the radios (`YesNoField`) and boxes (`FormField`) carry both. Cost: if axe wants it on the checkboxes, add it and say so.
- **`YesNoField` prints Yes first;** E/F prints offset as No, then Yes. Left as Task 5 built it.
- **Remove** shows on every real row. The one blank row the step shows with no rows has none. Removing the last row leaves no rows.
- **`DATE_INCURRED_MAX_LENGTH`** is declared in `claimNames.ts` with the value Task 1 recorded for R21, on one line as `export const DATE_INCURRED_MAX_LENGTH = <integer>;`, and `MAX_NOTICE_PARTIES` the same way, so Task 11's pin can read both. Task 11 pins them against `DebtInfo`'s. Cost: a second place to change each until then.
- **`account_last_4`** (B12): a row with one shows `ACCOUNT_SAVED` under the account box.

**Copy.** Every sentence is exact, and goes in the step that adds it (Step 4).

    - `HOME_LINE_FIRST = 'First mortgage'` and `HOME_LINE_ADDITIONAL = 'Additional mortgage, such as a home equity loan'` (8.2). These are DigniFi's words, not printed text: a comment says they are cut from Schedule J lines 4 and 5, which print "first mortgage payments" and "Additional mortgage payments for your residence, such as home equity loans", cut to the loan since the filer answers about the loan.
    - `SECURED_PREFILLED_NOTE = 'We filled this in from the kind of amount owed. Change it if it is wrong.'` (1.5). It shows under the secured radios while the radios show an answer and `_securedPressed` is not true (the answer was filled in from the kind and the filer hasn't pressed it), and it hides once the filer presses either radio. A loaded non-draft row is pressed, so it never shows there.
    - `DEBTS_STEP_WHY = 'Your answers here fill in Schedules D and E/F, the court forms that list who you owe.'` (5.2). The step shows it under its intro paragraph. Every required control on the step sits under it, so it is the reason for them; the address help (`ADDRESS_HELP`) is the one per-field reason, and no other field gets its own.

  **`DebtsStep.test.tsx`.** Add a harness that holds the rows and `noDebts` like the wizard, and pass `noDebts`, `onNoDebtsChange` and `assets` in the six badge tests (a badge test left out crashes at runtime on an undefined `assets`):

```tsx
function Harness({ initial = [], assets = [], onChange = noop, onValidity = noop }: {
  initial?: DebtRow[]; assets?: WizardRow<AssetInfo>[];
  onChange?: (rows: DebtRow[]) => void; onValidity?: (valid: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<DebtRow[]>(initial);
  const [noDebts, setNoDebts] = useState(false);
  return (
    <DebtsStep rows={rows} onRowsChange={(next) => { setRows(next); onChange(next); }}
      noDebts={noDebts} onNoDebtsChange={setNoDebts} assets={assets} onValidationChange={onValidity} />
  );
}
const status = () => screen.getByRole('status');
const field = (name: string) => document.querySelector(`[name="${name}"]`) as HTMLInputElement;
```
